### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Futures top-left instrument cluster, to the owner's Bybit reference (`claude/futures-topbar-pair-cluster`)

- **CUT FROM FRESH `main` AT `9bf0e49`.** PR #141 (Copy Trading) is still open and was not involved; nothing from it is in this branch.
- **THE REFERENCE, AND WHAT OURS ACTUALLY WAS.** The owner's package holds two captures: production `/futures` and Bybit's `BTCUSDT`. Bybit builds that corner as ONE block — list button, a large coin mark, then a two-line identity (pair over asset name) — set tight and centred as a unit. Ours had the same parts as three loose children of the header's flex row, sharing the header's own 20–24px gap, with a 20px mark and a single 15px line. That is why it read as items on a line rather than a block.
- **WHAT CHANGED, measured in Chromium before → after at 1440.** Mark **20×20 → 28×28**. Pair **15px/600 → 19px/650**. A second line appeared: the asset's own name at 11px tertiary. The three parts moved inside `.pair-cluster`, whose gap is its own 10px against the header's — so the button-to-mark gap is **21px → 10px** while the gap from the cluster to the first statistic is 19px at 1440 and 57px at 1920. That ratio is the whole difference between "four evenly spaced things" and "a block with air around it".
- **THE CAPTION IS REAL, never invented.** It comes from `useAssetMetadata`, the same batched catalogue store `CryptoIcon` already reads — both asks land in ONE request inside the store's 50ms window, so the caption costs no extra network. A symbol the catalogue does not cover renders an **empty** caption, not a guess and not a dash pretending to be a name; `.pair-asset` carries `min-height` so the pair above it does not shift when the name arrives. Both states are pinned by tests.
- **VERTICAL ALIGNMENT, by construction.** The identity is a flex column at `line-height:1.2` with no margins, so its optical centre lands on the mark's; the caret is `align-self:center` against the stack rather than the row. Nothing uses `baseline` — a 28px image and a 19px text baseline do not agree, which is exactly how the old row drifted. The browser run asserts every part's centre is within **2px** of the cluster's.
- **SCOPED TO FUTURES.** `.pair-selector`, `.pair-name` and `.pair-markets-btn` are shared with Spot and CFD; every new rule carries `.futures-reference`. Spot is **measured**, not assumed: no cluster, no caption, pair still 15px, no overflow, no page errors. Worth recording — Spot's selector has no asset mark at all, only name + caret, so "the mark did not grow" is not a claim that can be made about it; the harness says so in a comment rather than asserting something false.
- **THE PUBLISHED SEARCH IS UNTOUCHED.** `futuresMarketSearch.test.ts` passes **20/20**: both entry points, one shared handler, `data-market-entry` on both, `aria-expanded` on both, the chooser's Escape/outside-click. The cluster wraps those controls; it does not rewire them.
- **Material files.** `frontend/src/components/FuturesTickerBar.tsx` (cluster wrapper, 28px mark, two-line identity, one metadata read), `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/TerminalAccountPanel.css` (one appended `.futures-reference`-scoped section), `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/futuresTickerHeader.test.ts` (dependency supplied, digest re-pinned, five new cluster tests), NEW `scripts/qa-futures-topbar-cluster.cjs`, evidence in `docs/qa/futures-topbar-cluster/`.
- **ONE GUARD RE-PINNED, bounded.** `futuresTickerHeader.test.ts` hashes the component's "reads" block; the caption's one display read lands inside it. Re-pinned from `27318636…a152e` to `4282c422…bb765`, with the new read asserted **by name** so the re-take cannot cover anything else, plus an assertion that nothing financial entered that line. The reads this guard exists to protect — mark price, index price, settled funding rate — are byte-unchanged. The suite's render harness also needed the new module supplied, which is what 18 of the 18 initial failures were; that is a fixture gap, not a weakened test.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No trading math, order execution, balances, P&L, order book, Wallet or engine #123. No figure in the header changed: price, mark, 24h, turnover, open interest and funding read exactly as before, and unknown is still «—».
- **TESTED.** Backend `tsc` PASS, frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS. Browser harness **PASS at 1920 / 1664 / 1440 / 1366 / 390** plus the Spot pass at 1440 — zero page errors, zero horizontal overflow at every width. Whole repo compared test by test against `main` (`9bf0e49`) in the same tree, both sides built: **127 failing before, 127 after — zero new, zero fixed**.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** No production check. All browser evidence is the local fixture harness: the prices in the screenshots are «—» because the fixture serves no market data, which is the honest empty state rather than a rendering fault. The caption was exercised with a catalogue that knows BTC and with one that knows nothing; a symbol whose real catalogue name is unusually long was not tested beyond the CSS ellipsis. Comparison against the reference is by composition, not a pixel diff against Bybit.
- **NOT MERGED**, as instructed.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Copy Trading: card availability, hidden trades, Ksenia's reported week, Nazar's avatar ring (`claude/copy-trading-cards-trades-ksenia-avatar`)

- **CUT FROM FRESH `main` AT `9bf0e49`**, which carries PR #140. Open PRs were checked first: **PR #92 ("Make Nazar and Ksenia paint instantly in Copy Trading") is still open on a base from 16.09** and was NOT reused — its branch predates everything since, and the cause found here is a different one. Nothing from it was duplicated.
- **1. THE CARDS — the cause is the retry rule, and it is not the cache.** Ruled the payload out first, empirically: driving the real `CopyPerformanceService` **day by day from 2026-09-06 to 2026-09-26** — the way production advances, one request at a time rather than one jump — and putting every day's summary through the client's own `validStrategy()` leaves **both strategies valid on every one of those days**. Not a validator rejection, not a shape drift. The defect is in `prefetch()`: it throttles on `lastAttempt`, which `refresh()` stamps when an attempt **starts**, so a request that started and then FAILED is indistinguishable from one that succeeded. Hover the nav link, let that request fail (a blip, a 503 from one instance, the 15s timeout), click through within thirty seconds → the page mounts, asks, is told it just asked, and **both cards read «Данные недоступны» with nothing in flight**. Now: a success may be reused for 30s, a failure may not be reused at all, and a 200 whose every section was rejected counts as a failure. Proven in Chromium: **1 request → 2**.
- **TWO GUARANTEES THAT ALREADY HELD, now pinned so they cannot regress**: a failing section keeps its last-good value, and one strategy failing never blanks the other, in either direction. I did not fix these; I proved and pinned them.
- **THE CARD NO LONGER PASSES A VERDICT EARLY.** It announced the terms missing while the very first request was still in flight. Waiting now says «Загрузка…» with `aria-busy`; «Данные недоступны» is kept for when the answer really is not coming.
- **DEVELOPER DIAGNOSTICS, no leakage.** A closed set of non-identifying labels on the client (`unauthenticated`, `network`, `timeout`, `http_error`, `malformed_envelope`, `server_unavailable`, `rejected_by_client`) and the real error logged **server-side only**. The wire still tells the visitor `temporarily_unavailable` and nothing else; nothing is rendered from the diagnosis; no token, account or server text enters either.
- **2. THE TRADES ARE WITHHELD ON THE SERVER, not hidden in the markup.** NEW `tradeHistoryVisibility.ts` strips the rows at the last step before the response is written. **Two endpoints leaked them, not one**: besides the marketplace and the per-strategy links, `/copy-trading/synthetic` predates all of this and returns the SAME strategy (`trader.id` is VX-001) with its whole trade array to any authenticated visitor — redacted on identical terms. Admin routes untouched: the full history stays server-side for the calculations and the owner's access.
- **ORDER IS THE GUARANTEE.** `withKseniaReportedTrade` merges an owner-reported row into the display window, so redaction runs LAST: fold in, then redact, then send. A test applies the overlay in the wrong order **on purpose** and shows the row returning — which is what makes the route ordering a claim rather than a hope. The reported trade's own `reportedPerformance` block goes too; one owner-reported execution is still an execution.
- **HIDDEN IS NOT ZERO.** Every aggregate is still computed from the COMPLETE history and still sent; a test asserts `tradeStats`, `analytics`, `economics`, `equityHistory`, `dailyResults`, `weekly`, `monthly` and `mainMarkets` are **identical before and after redaction**. The profile shows «Информация о сделках скрыта» under a struck-through eye and keeps the real count beside it (**«106 закрытых»** in the browser run). It does NOT say "available to subscribers" — no server rule makes that true — and a client-side «Копирую»/«Избранное» flag never reaches this code.
- **THE VALIDATOR WAS TAUGHT THE DIFFERENCE, not weakened.** `trades: []` on a strategy with 465 trades looks exactly like a truncated response, so the omission is DECLARED (`tradeVisibility`) and checked strictly. **Nothing financial is relaxed**: every ROI, PnL, gross profit/loss, net total, count, drawdown and volatility is still required finite in every period. The declaration can excuse only the **holding-time** aggregate — a duration, not a financial figure — for the periods it names.
- **CURRENT TRADES WERE NEVER EXPOSED**: the payload carries only closed trades and there is no open-position surface anywhere in Copy Trading. The user's own orders and fills are untouched — redaction keys on the strategy identity and is applied only on those two routes.
- **3. KSENIA: 61.9% EXPLAINED, NOT APPLIED.** Today is a **Saturday**, so the engine's Sunday-start UTC week (**2026-09-13 → 2026-09-19**) and the rolling 7D window (> 2026-09-12) hold the same seven dates — **only today**; by Sunday the week holds one day and 7D still holds seven, which a test shows. The week drew **regime 0.216052** → the 10–30% band → a weekly target of **12.7094%**. The already-published 16.09 owner-reported trade adds **7.8pp**, giving **20.5094%** — the "around 20%" in the report, and exactly what `economics.periods['7D'].roi`, `analytics.roi7` and the weekly row read. **61.9% is 41.39pp above that.**
- **THERE IS NO 20% CEILING.** The top band is 45–70% and the same generator drew **69.6216%** for the week before. 61.9% is inside what this model produces; it is simply not what this week drew.
- **NO SCHEDULER.** Nothing in `src/` schedules the model; it advances inside `CopyPerformanceService.get()`, i.e. when the API is called. Tests cover repeat reads on one UTC day (one state, no extra days), a two-day gap (caught up, earlier days unchanged), and concurrent first requests (one history).
- **NOTHING COUNTED TWICE.** The 16.09 trade folds in exactly once however often the day is served: the overlay deep-copies, so the service's cached response stays clean. Five requests give an identical 09-16, and the cached response is still 1,754 USDT lighter than the published one.
- **WHAT WAS RECORDED.** 61.9% sits BESIDE the model in `kseniaReportedWeek.ts`: period bounds, timezone, provenance `OWNER_REPORTED`, the modeled 12.7094% and published 20.5094% it is reported against, and — **the owner confirmed this when asked** — an explicit `includesReportedTradeOf20260916: false`, so the two can never be silently merged. **No history was retrofitted**: no invented trade, price or daily curve; no balance, AUM, follower PnL, fee, ROI or equity point moved (asserted field by field). The modeled figures stay `SYNTHETIC_REVIEW`.
- **4. THE AVATAR RING.** Nazar's card already carried `trader-card-vip`, so the shared rule already entitled it to Ksenia's ring — but a later, more specific rule in `CopyTradingRefinement.css` replaced it with a 1px #9b8965 hairline and switched the glow off with `box-shadow: none`. That was the whole cause. Measured in Chromium, before → after: `1px solid rgb(155,137,101)` / `none` → **`2px solid rgb(202,141,53)` / `rgba(247,166,0,.12) 0 0 0 3px`**, identical to Ksenia's. Values copied from hers rather than re-chosen. Verified **with a real decodable photo in place and with initials**; the photo is a child at `inset:0`, inside the border box, so it cannot paint over the ring. Size, padding and card geometry untouched; no other trader affected.
- **Material files.** NEW `src/services/copyTrading/tradeHistoryVisibility.ts`, NEW `src/services/copyTrading/kseniaReportedWeek.ts`, `src/api/routes/copyPerformance.ts`, `src/api/routes/syntheticCopyTrading.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/copyMarketplaceStore.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/useCopyMarketplace.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/syntheticCopyTrading.ts`, `frontend/src/pages/copy-trading-bolt/components.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/copy-trading-bolt/CopyTradingRefinement.css`, NEW tests `tradeHistoryVisibility.test.ts` / `kseniaReportedWeek.test.ts` / `copyMarketplaceRetry.test.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/copyDepositUx.test.ts`, NEW `scripts/qa-copy-cards-trades-avatar.cjs`, evidence in `docs/qa/copy-cards-trades-avatar/`.
- **PRESERVED FROM THE OTHER AGENT.** Codex's order-book work and `TerminalPremium.css` are untouched. `futuresDepth.ts` was not read or changed.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No Futures, order book, Wallet math or engine #123. No production data. No balances, AUM, follower PnL or fees changed.
- **ONE GUARD RE-PINNED, covering two changes, both read first.** `copyDepositUx.test.ts`'s `CopyButton` hash was **already stale on `main`** (pinned `cd2ed289…`, actual `7f6c10d0…`). Diffed against `c04d930`, where it was last valid: the drift is exactly one line, from when Ksenia became a featured strategy (`trader.id === nazarTrader.id` → `(… || trader.id === 'VX-KSENIA')`). Re-pinned to `14f46f69…` with that history in the comment, plus this branch's loading state. What the guard protects is unchanged and still asserted: the copy action is still `toggleFollowing(trader.id)` and the fee still `trader.performanceFee * 100`.
- **TESTED.** Backend `tsc` PASS, frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS. Browser harness **PASS at 1440 and 390**, zero findings, zero page errors — covering cold open, the failed-prefetch-then-mount regression, a failing refresh, one-section failure both ways, hard reload, account switch, the withheld-history block, the statistics tab's surviving aggregates, and **every copy-trading response body scanned for executions on the captured wire bytes** (5 responses, 0 executions). Whole repo compared test by test against `main` (`9bf0e49`) in the same tree, **both sides built**: **127 failing before, 126 after — zero new, one fixed** (4 202 tests).
- **A NOTE ON THAT BASELINE.** My first comparison looked like +13; it was unfair. `registerWalletTailwindOwnership.test.ts`'s build-output case only runs when `frontend/dist` exists, which my tree had and the base worktree did not. Built the base and re-ran: the `isSelected` failure **reproduces identically on `main`** and is not mine.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** No production check. All browser evidence is the local fixture harness — real compiled router and service, in-memory database, no market feed, no credentials. The avatar photo used in QA is a generated gradient, not the owner's real uploaded image. The redaction is proven on these two routes and on the captured wire; no exhaustive sweep of every other endpoint was performed. Whether the owner's 61.9% is reproducible from any source outside the owner's own report is unknown — it is recorded as reported, not verified.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Plain language: the exchange stops naming its own plumbing (`claude/plain-language`)

- **THE BRIEF, AND THE STANDING RULE.** The owner is taking the exchange to investors and asked for every technical phrase to come off the design — WebSockets, APIs, balances described as demo, synthetic data, and the rest — plus a standing instruction to never leave such wording in future work and to remove any noticed while doing something else. This entry covers the sweep; the guard below covers the standing part.
- **CUT FROM `main` AT `135d8be`, then merged `origin/main` `9bf0e49`** (PRs #136–#140: order-book reconnect, market search, bottom panel, funding label / wallet chip / contrast, chart toolbar). The merge was clean. Everything those five PRs added was re-scanned afterwards and none of it needed a correction.
- **FOUND BY SCANNING, NOT BY BROWSING.** Two scanners: one over the seven dictionaries, one over every rendered string and JSX text node in `frontend/src`. They agreed on the list: **43 dictionary strings in 7 languages** (7 keys × ~6–7 locales, from the earlier pass) plus **14 more keys this pass**, and **15 hardcoded strings** in components and legal copy.
- **WHAT CHANGED, BY SURFACE.**
  - *Terminal and market connection.* `connection.lost` no longer names the WebSocket (ru: «Связь с рынком прервана. Восстанавливаем…»). `analytics.liquidityMapPending` drops the «Синтетические данные» sentence. `home.markets.cfdNotConfigured` says prices are unavailable instead of that a provider is unconfigured.
  - *Money and reserves.* `settings.reservesDisclaimer` **keeps both material claims — self-reported, and no third-party sign-off — and drops only the database and public-API mechanics**. `settings.reserves.liabilities` was «Обязательства (в базе)» / "Liabilities (database)" and is now «Обязательства перед пользователями» / "User liabilities". `settings.reservesEmpty` loses «деплойменте»; `deposit.noneConfigured` loses the two env-var names; `settings.deposits.incomingLoadError` loses «API». `wallet.chartNoHistoryBody` says daily account value rather than equity snapshots.
  - *Homepage.* The hero terminal's feed badge was `Stale` / `6h snapshot` and is now `Delayed` / `Updated every 6h`; its footer, order book, trades strip and chart note follow. `globalHeroCopy` en/zh say market data rather than snapshots. The candle label dropped the defensive word "real". English `analytics.stale` / `catalogue.stale` / `futures.orderError.staleQuote` fall in line with the six locales that already said "out of date".
  - *Earn, admin, legal.* The Earn page drops "server-side" and «Демо-данные не используются»; the admin deposit hint drops "backend"; the admin test-balance section drops «(песочница)». Two half-English labels became Russian. In all seven languages the privacy and About pages say passwords are stored as **irreversible cryptographic hashes** instead of naming bcrypt, and the market-data sentence says quotes come from live exchange venues instead of denying that they are hardcoded.
  - *One fictional trader.* The copy-trading marketplace listed a trader called **SandboxAlpha**; it is now SundaAlpha (same id, initials, region and every figure).
- **TWO LABELS CHANGE WHAT THE SCREEN DISCLOSES, NOT JUST HOW IT READS — FLAGGED, NOT HIDDEN.** `futures.demoAvailable` went from «Демо баланс» to «Доступно для торговли», and the private terminal's two mode badges («Симуляция», «▣ Демо трейдинг») are now «Приватный счёт». An account pinned to the NATIVE engine — which `futuresExecution.tsx` documents as *no real order, no real wallet write* — now carries **no visible marker that its balance is simulated**. That is a disclosure decision, not a wording one, and it is the owner's to make. Nothing about the engine, the ledger or the routing changed; only the words.
- **WHAT DELIBERATELY STAYED.** API keys keep the word "API" everywhere — it is the name of a product the user creates and revokes on a settings page of its own, not a leak. The admin console keeps saying «Тестовый баланс» plainly: it is staff-only, and making credited test funds read like a normal balance would be the dangerous edit, not the honest one. Both exemptions are written into the guard with the reason.
- **NEW GUARD, AND IT WAS PROVED TO BITE.** `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/plainLanguage.test.ts` reads the seven dictionaries and every rendered string in `frontend/src` and fails on the vocabulary above, skipping identifiers, translation keys, CSS class lists, enum members and code fragments so it flags copy and not code. Before leaving it green I planted two violations — a dictionary value («Демо-режим: синтетические данные из BITCOIN_TREASURY_ADDRESS») and a component string ("WebSocket demo snapshot") — and confirmed it reported both, then reverted them.
- **TWO HARNESSES UPDATED, NEITHER WEAKENED.** `homeSixHourSnapshot.test.ts` pinned the badge literal; it now pins the new one, and what the guard exists to prove — that the badge states the six-hour cadence on its face, which a socket-fed page could not say — is unchanged. `scripts/qa-home-snapshot-reload.cjs` expects the new badge text.
- **A HARNESS THAT WAS ALREADY BROKEN, REPAIRED IN PASSING.** `scripts/qa-futures-demo-activation.cjs` read four summary rows by label. Three of those labels had drifted before this work («Маржинальный баланс», «Начальная маржа», «Поддерживающая маржа» vs the «Баланс маржи», «Используемая НМ», «Используемая ПМ» the panel actually renders), so every read returned null and the run died on the first one. The needles are corrected; the rows, the figures and every assertion are the same.
- **Material files.** `frontend/src/lib/i18n/locales/{ru,en,zh,es,hi,ja,ko}.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/legalContent.ts`, `frontend/src/pages/BankingPage.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/admin/{AdminDepositsPage,AdminUserDetailPage}.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/private-trading/{PrivatePositions,PrivateTradingPage}.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/copy-trading-bolt/traders.ts`, `frontend/src/pages/home/{SapphireTerminal,HomeSapphireTape,globalHeroCopy}.*`, NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/plainLanguage.test.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/homeSixHourSnapshot.test.ts`, `scripts/qa-home-snapshot-reload.cjs`, `scripts/qa-futures-demo-activation.cjs`, evidence in `docs/qa/plain-language/`.
- **COPY ONLY.** No component logic, no CSS, no layout, no trading math, no execution, no balances, no P&L, no wallet, no order book, no market-data behaviour, no routing. Codex's `TerminalPremium.css`, the order-book work and `futuresDepth.ts` were not read or changed. Engine PR #123 is untouched.
- **TESTED.** Frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS. Whole frontend suite compared test by test against `origin/main` (`9bf0e49`) in a worktree at the same commit, both with `frontend/dist` built so the same suites run on each side: **107 failing before, 107 after — zero new, zero fixed** (1 872 tests on the head, 1 864 on the base; the difference is this change's own 8 new passing tests). Browser: `qa-native-demo-browser` **29/29, zero errors**; `qa-home-snapshot-reload` **PASS, zero findings, zero page errors**, badge read back as `Updated every 6h`; `qa-futures-demo-activation` **79/84**, with the renamed row read correctly before and after activation.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** No production check. All browser evidence is the local fixture harness — no market feed, no credentials. The five failing activation checks were not chased: one is a further drifted label («Тип маржи») that this sweep did not touch, and the rest need outbound market data that this sandbox blocks, so I cannot say from here whether they pass in CI. The seven languages were rewritten by a native-speaker standard I cannot verify for hi/ja/ko/zh — the meaning is preserved from the string each replaced, but a speaker should read them. Screenshots were taken at 1440 only, and the homepage evidence is the harness report rather than a crop, because the fixture server does not render the marketing hero.
- **NOT MERGED**, as instructed.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Customer-facing wording and error audit before the investor demo (`claude/customer-error-wording`, issue #144)

- **THE BRIEF.** Every customer-facing surface, in every failure state, must read in the customer's own language and must not print the server's. Equally: nothing honest may be lost — no faked success, no faked live data, no hidden unavailability, no unknown turned into `0`, no business refusal removed, last-good data still visible during a refresh, success only after the server confirms it.
- **CUT FROM CURRENT `main` AT `e7abb0f`**, fresh-fetched. PR #123 was not read or touched.
- **THE ACTUAL PROBLEM, NAMED.** `ApiError.message` is whatever a route put in `body.error`, and across this backend that is five different things at once: a sentence for a customer, a sentence for a developer (`Order not found or not cancellable`), a caught exception's own message, a flattened Zod report, or the client's own `Request failed (500)`. Twenty-one display sites rendered it directly and could not tell them apart. Three of those printed things that should never be on a screen at all: an **endpoint path** (`Start setup first with /account/2fa/setup`), an **operator's private note** (production has one reading `Аккаунт заблокирован: Isolated fixture block`), and in `AssetLedger` **any JavaScript exception**, via `error instanceof Error ? error.message`.
- **THE FIX IS ONE BOUNDARY, NOT TWENTY-ONE PATCHES.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/customerError.ts` answers a failure in this order: a `code` the caller has wording for, then one the shared table knows; the server's sentence matched against the phrases these routes actually emit; the transport status for the two cases that need no sentence (throttled, server broke); the caller's own localized fallback. There is deliberately **no pass-through**: a well-formed English sentence is still English in a Russian interface, so it is answered rather than echoed, and the original goes to the console so the gap is visible to us and not to the customer.
- **THE REFUSALS ARE KEPT, IN FULL, IN SEVEN LANGUAGES.** 40 new `serverError.*` keys × 7 locales cover every refusal these routes make: not enough of a named asset (`Недостаточно BTC на балансе` — the ticker is carried through, because "not enough" without naming the coin sends the customer to check the wrong one), an order that can no longer be cancelled, a wrong password, a wrong code, a pending verification, a throttled request, a closed registration. Nothing was softened into "try again".
- **THE THREE CONFIRMED FINDINGS FROM THE ISSUE.**
  1. *Wallet collateral.* «Нет цены для {assets}. Баланс показан как нижняя граница.» → «Оценка неполная: для {assets} пока нет цены, и эти активы не вошли в сумму.» in all seven. The three facts survive intact: the estimate is incomplete, the named assets are the reason, and they are **missing from the total rather than counted as zero**.
  2. *Futures order errors.* `futuresOrderErrors.ts` no longer returns `error.message` for an unmapped code, and now ALSO reads `body.code` off the real account's `ApiError` — which recovers refusals that previously fell to the generic line. `INVALID_PROTECTION` is newly mapped, so «stopLoss must be below the current mark price for a LONG position» reads as «Проверьте цену TP/SL относительно текущей цены.» — same fact, no English, no request field the trader never typed.
  3. *privateTradingApi.* `privateExplanation` and `privateErrorText` are closed against raw text with every business branch kept. One ordering bug fixed while there: prose matching ran before the status check, so a 500 carrying `TypeError: … (reading 'balance')` came back as «Недостаточно средств» — **a refusal the account never made**. Status is now read first.
- **WHAT ELSE WAS FOUND AND FIXED.** 16 further display sites across Wallet, Spot, Futures, Settings, Support, Login and Register; `PrivateOrderTicket` and `PrivateResultCardDialog`, which showed a local JS fault's message; `useNativeDemo`, which set any `Error.message` into page state. `RegisterPanel`'s local `useServerErrorLocalizer` — three mappings and a `return message` pass-through — is gone, its three mappings absorbed into the shared table with the same `register.error.*` wording.
- **WHAT WAS DELIBERATELY LEFT.** The **admin console** keeps the server's own words on all seven of its sites: its readers are the operators who need them, and issue #144 says so. **Earn/Banking** already has a clean code-keyed boundary (`bankingErrorText`) with no leak, but its wording is Russian-only in a seven-language interface — a real gap, out of this issue's scope, not fixed.
- **ONE FINDING REPORTED, NOT FIXED — IT IS THE OWNER'S CALL.** Copy Trading's Copy button calls `toggleFollowing(trader.id)`, which writes a **browser-local list and nothing else**, then immediately claims «Вы копируете {name}» with «Комиссия за результат X%. Средства остаются на вашем счёте VOLTEX.» No request is sent and no server confirms anything. By this issue's own rule that is a fake success, and before an investor demo it is the highest-value item in this list — but the fix is either wiring a real subscription (out of scope, and trading logic) or renaming a feature, and neither is a wording decision I should take alone.
- **REGRESSION TESTS.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/customerFacingErrors.test.ts`, 62 cases. Nine hostile payloads — a raw HTTP status line, an HTML error page carrying a stack and a connection string, a stack-like string, an unknown engine code, an arbitrary `error.message`, a flattened Zod report, `Request failed (500)`, a bare runtime fault, a non-`Error` rejection — are each driven through all three display paths and asserted absent from the result. Twenty real refusals are asserted to survive with their meaning. A repository sweep fails if any file outside the three boundary modules and the admin console reads `.message` off a caught error; **it was proved to bite** by re-introducing the `OrderForm` leak and watching it name the file and line.
- **BROWSER QA.** NEW `scripts/qa-customer-error-wording.cjs`: `/futures`, `/wallet`, `/copy-trading`, `/trade` and `/settings`, each in **normal, failing and partial** state, loaded and then reloaded in each — **200/200 checks, zero page errors**. The failing state answers every request 502 with an HTML error page containing a stack, a `DATABASE_URL` and an engine code; the partial state answers 200 with the figures null, which is where a zero would be a lie. Each screen is checked against ten shapes of banned vocabulary plus a false-success pattern. **The first version of this harness was worthless and was rewritten**: with thin fixtures every page fell to the error boundary, so all it ever checked was the boundary's own text. The check is now "the real screen rendered, not the error boundary", and the fixtures are shaped like the real routes.
- **THREE GUARDS REPOINTED AT THE MEANING, NONE WEAKENED.** `orderPanelNavCleanup.test.ts` and `walletUxRefinement.test.ts` pinned the literal «нижняя граница»; they now assert the facts that phrase carried (incomplete, named, not in the sum, not zero) and that the jargon does not come back. `scripts/qa-wallet-unified-account.cjs` likewise — note it is **not run by CI**, so that repair is only exercised when someone runs it by hand.
- **TWO GUARDS PINNED THE LEAK AND WERE FLIPPED, WITH THE REVERSAL WRITTEN DOWN.** `futuresOrderErrors.test.ts` asserted that an unmapped code shows «серверный текст»; it now asserts the fallback plus a console warning naming the code. `futuresPositionProtection.test.ts` asserted that "any other failure still shows the server's own words"; it now asserts the localized sentence and the absence of the English one. That suite also **stubbed the localizer as the identity on the fallback**, so it could not tell a mapped refusal from an unmapped one — it now loads the real module, as do `spotButtonLabels.test.ts` and `terminalAccountPanel.test.ts`.
- **FOUR HASHES RE-TAKEN, EACH FOR A READ CHANGE.** `wallet-v3/WithdrawModal.tsx` and `TransferModal.tsx` (one display line each, success path untouched), `AuthPage.tsx` and `RegisterPanel.tsx`. `plainLanguage.test.ts` now skips `lib/customerError.ts`, which holds no copy — its literals are the **server's** sentences, listed so each can be answered; the wording it actually shows is checked against that same vocabulary in all seven languages by the new suite.
- **Material files.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/customerError.ts`, NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/customerFacingErrors.test.ts`, NEW `scripts/qa-customer-error-wording.cjs`, NEW `.github/workflows/customer-error-wording.yml`; `frontend/src/lib/futuresOrderErrors.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/privateTradingApi.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/i18n/locales/{ru,en,zh,es,hi,ja,ko}.ts`; `frontend/src/components/{FuturesOrderForm,FuturesPositionProtection,FuturesPositionsPanel,FuturesTransferModal,OpenOrdersPanel,OrderForm,SupportWidget,WithdrawModal}.tsx`; `frontend/src/pages/AuthPage.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/register/RegisterPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/settings-arctic/{ApiSection,EditProfileModal,SecuritySection,VerificationSection}.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/wallet-v3/{AssetLedger,TransferModal,WithdrawModal}.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/private-trading/{PrivateOrderTicket,PrivateResultCardDialog,useNativeDemo}.tsx`; the six guards above and `scripts/qa-wallet-unified-account.cjs`.
- **PRESERVED FROM THE OTHER AGENT.** Codex's order-book work, `TerminalPremium.css` and `futuresDepth.ts` were not read or changed. Engine PR #123 is untouched.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No trading math, balances, P&L, margin, execution, order matching, wallet ledger or database schema. No production data. Every change is the text shown when something fails, plus the tables that choose it.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Order book: a quiet cadence, last-good through a reconnect, and execution kept out of it (`claude/orderbook-ux`)

- **THE BRIEF.** The visible book must keep its last real snapshot on screen, refresh roughly every 30 s, refresh quietly when the tab comes back, stop raising a reconnect banner on a normal return, never blank during a refresh or reconnect, never show synthetic depth, and hold a stale threshold longer than the refresh interval — without heavy polling. And the one that must not bend: a manual MARKET open or close never waits on that cadence and never reads the browser's copy of the book.
- **CUT FROM CURRENT `main` AT `87f1d83`**, fresh-fetched. PR #123 and the Futures engine math were not touched.
- **WHAT #122 ACTUALLY WAS, AND WHAT WAS TAKEN.** `main` has moved **149 commits** since #122 was cut, and the PR is `dirty`. Read file by file rather than merged: `OrderBookPanel.tsx`, `spotOrderBook.ts` and `qa-orderbook-motion.cjs` are **untouched on `main` since #122's base**, so its Spot work was still entirely unapplied; `index.css`, `TerminalAccountPanel.css`, `FuturesReferenceBook.tsx` and the pages had all moved on.
  - **TAKEN — the calm-motion core, and only that.** Rows keyed by **depth slot** instead of price; the per-row pulse (`useRowFlash`, `FLASH_DURATION_MS`, `.book-row-flash-*` and its two keyframes) **deleted, not disabled**; the depth bar moved from an animated `width` to a right-anchored `scaleX` transform. This is what serves the brief: a price key makes React destroy and rebuild every row whose price moved, which is both the live-ladder shimmer and the reason a refresh looked like a rebuild.
  - **DROPPED AS STALE.** #122's `TerminalAccountPanel.css` block is ~120 lines of **hardcoded hex** (`#16181e`, `#eaecef`, `#848e9c`, `#f6465d`, `#0ecb81`) behind four-deep `.trade-terminal` specificity stacks. `main` has since merged the premium-palette **token layer** over the same selectors; porting that block would have quietly undone it. Its `index.css` diff (+1209) is a **ticker-strip** change, not an order-book one. Also dropped: the Spot panel-chrome rework, the `markPrice`/`rb-mark` centre addition, `VISIBLE_LEVELS_PER_SIDE` 15→30, the `spotGroupSteps` 1-2-5-10-50 → 1-10-100-1000-10000 ladder, and `docs/qa/orderbook/*` evidence taken against the old base. None of those is in the brief, and each would have fought merged work.
- **ONE PLACE DECIDES FRESHNESS.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/bookFreshness.ts`: refresh 30 s, stale 90 s, unavailable 300 s, reconnect grace 35 s. The rule is written down and now guarded — stale must outlast the refresh by a whole multiple, because if the two are close an ordinary gap between refreshes reads as "out of date" every cycle, and a trader who learns to ignore that will ignore the real one.
- **THE NUMBERS THAT WERE WRONG, AND WHY THEY MATTERED.** `futuresDepth` had `STALE_AFTER_MS` 12 s and `UNAVAILABLE_AFTER_MS` 60 s against a **one-second** fallback poll — sixty backend reads a minute per subscribed contract, for a display panel. Slowing the poll without moving those two would have made a healthy 30-second feed declare itself stale at 12 s and blank itself at 60 s. All three now come from the shared module. Spot's `TradePage` had a **2-second** interval against a 4-second silence window; it is now one read per 30 s, and a healthy socket suppresses it entirely.
- **LAST-GOOD, AND THE ONE PLACE THAT MAY BLANK.** `TradePage` emptied the ladder on every effect re-run; it now does so only when the pair actually changed — BTC depth must never sit under an ETH header, but a reconnect is not a market change. `futuresDepth` already served `lastGood` through a reconnect and that is unchanged, including the deliberate `unavailable` path that drops levels once they are no longer a price: empty there means "we do not know", never zero.
- **THE TAB RETURN.** `TradePage` re-reads once on `visibilitychange`, quietly — nothing is cleared and nothing is raised. `ConnectionBanner`'s grace went from **2 s** to the shared 35 s, and a return now takes a banner down and re-arms rather than raising one. A connection that is genuinely still down is still reported; the grace delays the message, it does not delete it. Both listeners are guarded with `typeof document !== 'undefined'`, matching the depth store's existing convention — that guard was found by a test running outside jsdom, and it is a real robustness fix, not a test accommodation.
- **EXECUTION WAS ALREADY SEPARATE, AND IS NOW PINNED.** Verified rather than assumed: the visible book mirrors a public venue for display, matching runs on the exchange's own server-side `OrderBook`, and no order payload carries a level from the browser — `placeFuturesOrder` sends symbol/side/type/price?/quantity/leverage/marginType/reduceOnly and nothing else, `candle` is `null` for every ordinary order, and no execution module imports a display-book module (`OrderForm`'s one import from `spotOrderBook` is a number formatter used on three display lines). NEW `orderBookFreshnessAndExecution.test.ts` pins all of it, so the slower cadence can never become an execution dependency.
- **PROVED, NOT ASSERTED — THE SAME HARNESS ON BOTH BUILDS.** NEW `scripts/qa-orderbook-ux.cjs` drives the real production bundle and measures behaviour rather than reading constants. On this branch **14/14, zero page errors**. Run against a build of `main` at `87f1d83` (`QA_DIST`), the baseline **fails three** — and they are exactly the three faults in the brief: **35 requests in 70 s** (against 2 here), the banner reading «Связь с рынком прервана. Восстанавливаем…» on a normal return, and rows removed from the DOM on return. The checks that already passed on `main` (last-good surviving a failed refresh) are reported as already-good, not claimed as a fix.
- **GUARDS: four suites updated, none weakened.** `connectionBanner` and `futuresDepth` had the old timings typed in as literals; both now read the thresholds from `bookFreshness`, so the numbers cannot drift from the rule, and two new banner cases cover the return. `cfdTerminal` and `connectionBanner` needed the real freshness module in their loader shims rather than a stub. `spotOrdersPresentation`'s shared-stylesheet fingerprint is re-taken for the depth-bar rule **with three assertions pinning that exact mechanic**, following the file's own convention so the re-take cannot cover a different edit.
- **Material files.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/bookFreshness.ts`, NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/orderBookFreshnessAndExecution.test.ts`, NEW `scripts/qa-orderbook-ux.cjs`; `frontend/src/components/{OrderBookPanel,ConnectionBanner}.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/TradePage.tsx`, `frontend/src/lib/futuresDepth.ts`, `frontend/src/index.css`, `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/TradeTerminal.css`, and the five guards above.
- **PRESERVED FROM THE OTHER AGENT.** Codex's `TerminalPremium.css` and `futuresDepth`'s protocol handling were not rewritten; the premium-palette token layer is intact, which is precisely why #122's hex block was dropped. Engine PR #123 untouched.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No trading math, balances, P&L, margin, execution, order matching, wallet ledger or schema. No aggregation, sequence handling, delta application or crossed-book guard changed.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — The visual mismatches issue #148 measured, and nothing else (`claude/visual-mismatches-148`)

- **SCOPE.** Only what the #148 review proved wrong in the render: the populated row rhythm, the table's inner horizontal scroll, the chart toolbar's second row, and the one CSS declaration that review proved dead. No redesign, no other surface. Cut from `main` at `03fc3e6` (the review's own SHA) and merged up to `52dce28` after PR #149 landed; the only conflict was this file, where both agents had appended an entry, and both are kept.
- **THE REVIEW'S STATED CAUSE FOR THE 72px ROW WAS WRONG, AND MEASURING FIRST CAUGHT IT.** #148 blamed `flex-wrap:wrap` on `.futures-position-actions`. In the render that element computes `flex-wrap: nowrap` and never wrapped. The real cause was the contract cell's margin line: «Марж. торговля **Изолированная** 10.00x» is 190px of ink against «Кросс»'s 143, and at 1440/1366 the column could not hold it on one line. Only the ISOLATED row was tall, which is why one row of three broke the rhythm.
- **ONE CHANGE FIXES THREE OF THE FOUR DEFECTS, AND IT IS NOT A CSS CHANGE.** `FuturesPositionsPanel` set cell `padding` INLINE. An inline declaration outranks every ordinary rule in every sheet, media query or not — only an `!important` one gets past it. So the terminal's narrow-width padding split in two: the `!important` rules below 1500 applied, the plain ones at 1600 and wider silently did not, and the table kept its widest padding on exactly the widths with no room for it. Removing the inline `padding` (the sheet already had rules for it) takes 1664 from **38px of inner scroll to 0** and 1440/1366 from **62/72/62 to 62/62/62**. Measured in isolation, with every other file at `main`.
- **CI CAUGHT WHAT THE DESKTOP MEASUREMENTS COULD NOT: THE HISTORY TABLE AT 390px.** `qa-large-numbers-browser.cjs` failed seven `large-history-*-390` checks with «Phone table unexpectedly lost its columns». The open positions table is `.futures-positions-table` and the terminal's sheets are full of rules for it; the closed-position history table below it has five columns, not ten, and carried **no class at all**, so nothing in any sheet reached it and the removed inline padding was the only padding it had. Measured at 390: 455–463px of table in a 388px panel, scrolling sideways through its columns — and without the padding, exactly 388, collapsed into the width it was given. It now has `.futures-history-table` and the same `10px 14px` / `8px 14px`, in `FuturesPositionParity.css`, the sheet the component imports itself. 58/58 large-number checks pass. The phone positions table still overflows its panel by 719px and its rows take the same 62/72/62 → 62/62/62 fix as the desktop widths.
- **THE LAST PIXEL AT 1366 WAS A DUPLICATED MARGIN.** The two close pills carried `margin-right:2px` on top of the row's own `gap:3px`; between them the gap already covers it, and outside them it merely padded the cell's right edge — 4px of that column's minimum for nothing. Folding it into the gap takes 1366 from `scrollWidth 1052 / clientWidth 1051` to `1051 / 1051`. Labels, their 11px size and both controls untouched.
- **A RECUT COLUMN-WIDTH MAP WAS TRIED AND MEASURED TO CHANGE NOTHING, SO IT IS NOT IN THIS PR.** Both percentage maps were rebuilt from rendered ink and the result was byte-identical inner-scroll numbers at 1280/1400/1440/1500/1536/1600/1664/1728. Under `table-layout: auto` a column's min-content is a floor the percentages cannot lower, so at these widths the map is inert. `table-layout: fixed` would make it authoritative, and was rejected: the native QA harness caught it clipping `+ Добавить` and «Лимитный/Рыночный», and the sheet's own comment already records why — «a clipped number is the one outcome that is never acceptable».
- **THE TOOLBAR NEVER NEEDED A COMPROMISE.** Its base rule was `flex-direction:column; height:70px`, with the single row switched on only by `@media(min-width:1600px)`. The row layout now applies at every width, and the chart gets its 28px back at 1440 and 1366. One row is only worth having if every control is IN it: at 1366 the strip was 61px short of its own contents, so below 1440 the interval and chart-type buttons give up 3px of side padding and the four indicator buttons 2px — 13 buttons, and `MACD` lands inside the strip instead of past its scrollable end. Nothing removed, nothing hidden, no label or type size changed, nothing above 1439 touched.
- **DEAD CSS: one declaration, the one #148 proved dead.** `th { font-size: 11.5px }` under 1600 never reached a heading — a later unconditional 12.5px outranks it at every width. Removed; the rule's padding, which is live, stays. No other rule was deleted: the seven padding rules #148 called dead were dead because of the INLINE style, and that is fixed at the cause above rather than by deleting the rules.
- **MEASURED, EVERY VIEWPORT, BEFORE AND AFTER** (before = `main` `52dce28`, after = this branch; populated panel, three positions, one ISOLATED with TP and SL; both sides built from source and measured by the same harness):

  | | 1920×1080 | 1664×900 | 1440×900 | 1366×768 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | row heights before | 62/62/62 | 62/62/62 | **62/72/62** | **62/72/62** |
  | row heights after | 62/62/62 | 62/62/62 | **62/62/62** | **62/62/62** |
  | table inner scroll before | 0 | **38px** | 0 | **1px** |
  | table inner scroll after | 0 | **0** | 0 | **0** |
  | toolbar before | 42px, 1 row | 42px, 1 row | **70px, 2 rows** | **70px, 2 rows** |
  | toolbar after | 42px, 1 row | 42px, 1 row | **42px, 1 row** | **42px, 1 row** |
  | page overflow | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 |
  | tabs / headings / values | 14 / 12.5 / 13.5 | 14 / 12.5 / 13.5 | 14 / 12.5 / 13.5 | 14 / 12.5 / 13.5 |
  | clipped cells | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 | 0 → 0 |

- **OFF-TARGET WIDTHS IMPROVE OR HOLD; NONE REGRESSES.** Inner scroll 1280 `87→83`, 1400 `25→25`, 1500 `101→59`, 1600 `102→60`, 1728 `0→0`, and the 72px row is gone at 1280/1400/1500 too. The residue at those in-between widths is pre-existing and is NOT closed by this PR — the four viewports the task named are.
- **ORDER BOOK UNCHANGED, CHECKED RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** Same level counts per side in the same state at all four viewports (13/8/8/5 under the harness's fixture), same refresh cadence, nothing in `bookFreshness`, `futuresDepth` or the panels touched.
- **Material files.** `frontend/src/components/FuturesPositionsPanel.tsx` (inline cell padding removed, history table given a class), `frontend/src/components/FuturesPositionParity.css` (that table's padding), `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/FuturesStudio.css` (toolbar one row at every width, narrow-width button padding), `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/TerminalStudio.css` (indicator button padding below 1440), `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/TerminalAccountPanel.css` (pill margin folded into the gap, dead heading size removed), plus the before/after screenshot pack in `docs/qa/visual-148/`.
- **PRESERVED FROM THE OTHER AGENT.** Codex's premium-palette token layer and `TerminalStudio.css` colours are untouched — the one line added there is a padding override inside a media query. PR #123 and the Futures engine untouched.
- **TESTED on the merged head.** Frontend `tsc --noEmit` PASS. `vite build` PASS. `scripts/qa-native-demo-browser.cjs` **28/28 PASS**, `scripts/qa-large-numbers-browser.cjs` **58/58 PASS**. Whole repository suite against a `main` `52dce28` worktree at the same commit, both sides built: **4 355 / 4 516 passed, 123 failing tests in 29 suites — identical on both sides, zero new, zero fixed.**
- **OBSERVED, NOT FIXED (pre-existing, out of scope).** `scripts/qa-native-demo-browser.cjs` rebuilds the frontend from the REPO ROOT, so `frontend/tailwind.config.js`'s relative `content` globs resolve to nothing and the eager `index-*.css` ships without the homepage utilities. Running the suite straight after that harness therefore fails `homepageTailwindUtilities` test 5 on a locally-correct tree. CI is unaffected (separate jobs); a local run needs `vite build` from `frontend/` first.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** No production check. Every number here is the local harness against the real production bundle with fixture data. A real account with longer tickers or more than three positions was not exercised.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No trading math, balances, P&L, margin, execution, order matching, order book cadence, wallet ledger or schema.


## Codex — 2026-09-19 — native MARKET hang after #149 (draft PR #152)

- Owner requested current main and no merge. Based on `78a5a04fa532c3612020e15964c38649416174cc`; functional commits `ff447d449cc3831961943934199eedfbbe9d7746` and `8f64e9761e9185b072988dd4ea7e7c70fe64ae2a`, branch `codex/native-market-request-deadline`.
- Read-only production revision 71 reproduces a CHECKPOINT_MISMATCH: an empty OBSERVE was appended inside a sealed historical prefix because the flat snapshot's time did not advance. Dropping only that last suffix reproduces the stored digest; verified replay of the suffix reproduces the financial snapshot exactly. Old fallback starts history at Aug 1; recovered replay requires zero history calls. Raw account data is not in Git. Evidence is `docs/qa/native-command-deadline/checkpoint-evidence.json`.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Copy Trading: the visible 61.9%, and the real cause of «Загрузка…» (`claude/copy-trading-ksenia-visible-and-loading`)

- **CUT FROM FRESH `main` AT `03fc3e6`** (PR #141 merged as `c9f3ab9`; #143, #145, #147 on top). Two open items only; nothing else in Copy Trading was touched.
- **1. KSENIA — MY EARLIER CALL WAS WRONG, and this corrects it.** PR #141 recorded 61.9% as metadata and the file said in so many words that *nothing downstream moves*. The owner asked for it to be the **visible** weekly result, and it now is: `analytics.roi7`, `economics.periods['7D'].roi` and the `weekly[]` row for 2026-09-13 all carry **61.9**, with provenance `OWNER_REPORTED`.
- **WRITING IT SERVER-SIDE WAS NOT ENOUGH, and that is worth recording.** The card reads `economics.periods['7D'].roi`, so it picked the figure up immediately — but the **profile re-derives** the window from the daily series in `selectSyntheticPeriod` and quietly computed straight past it. So the payload now **declares** the override (`reportedWeeks[].appliedToVisibleWeeklyRoi`) and the selector honours it, for the `7D` window and nothing else. 30D / 90D / ALL derive exactly as before.
- **MEASURED IN THE BROWSER:** Ksenia's card at 7Д reads **+61.9%**, and the profile readout reads **`ROI · 7D +61.9%`**. Nazar at the same period reads +81.7% — untouched.
- **WHAT IT COSTS, said plainly.** No trade, price, daily curve or P&L was invented to justify the figure, and the engine's history is byte-identical (asserted field by field over `equityHistory`, `dailyResults`, `aumHistory`, `followers`, `tradeStats`, `trades`, `monthly`, `traderEarnings365`). **The consequence is that the weekly ROI is the owner's number while the PnL beside it is still the engine's** — the profile shows `+61.9%` next to `+24 631 USDT`. They do not reconcile, they are not meant to, and the payload says which is which.
- **IT IS ANCHORED TO THE REPORTED WEEK.** Applied only while the model is inside 2026-09-13 → 2026-09-19 UTC. From Sunday the rolling 7D window no longer describes that week, so 7D returns to the model and the reported figure stays on the `weekly[]` row it belongs to. Carrying one reported week forward into weeks that have not happened would be inventing data.
- **2. THE LOADING HANG — ROOT CAUSE, and it is not validation.** First ruled the payload out: the exact redacted shape production serves (empty `trades`, `tradeVisibility`, no `reportedPerformance`) passes `validStrategy` for both strategies; a test asserts it. **The cause is that `settled` was only ever set on two paths — the response handler and the catch — while four ways out of an attempt skipped both**, leaving the skeleton up with nothing in flight behind it and nothing scheduled but a 60s timer and a window-focus listener. On a page the viewer is already looking at, neither may ever fire.
- **The two that actually hang, reproduced by failing tests first:** (a) **`refresh()` with no session returns immediately** — a token that resolves a moment after the route paints leaves the store unsettled for ever, and `checkSession()` only swapped state without scheduling a fetch; (b) **a response voided by a session change was discarded** and the new session was left with nothing in flight. Both fixed: no session now settles (nothing is coming, and that is a verdict), a token arriving after mount triggers a fetch, and a voided response re-asks for whoever is logged in now.
- **Now structural:** one `markSettled()` is the single place the wait ends, so «Загрузка…» can only mean "asked, not yet answered".
- **PRODUCTION-LIKE BROWSER QA, every scenario the owner listed.** Real compiled router, `CopyPerformanceService`, `redactTradeHistory`, `withKseniaReportedWeek` and `requireAuth`; in-memory database; no `DATABASE_URL`, no production secret, no writes. **PASS with zero findings** across: cold load (both cards live), Ksenia's weekly figure on card and profile, route leave/return, hard reload, **failed prefetch → mount (1 request → 2)**, **token arriving after mount**, a failing refresh (last-good kept), and **a request that never answers → honest «Данные недоступны» after the client's own 15s abort, not an endless skeleton**.
- **#141's hidden-trades policy is untouched** — no validator relaxation, no change to `redactTradeHistory`, and the QA still drives the redacted payload end to end.
- **Material files.** `frontend/src/lib/copyMarketplaceStore.ts` (`markSettled`, the three closed paths), `frontend/src/lib/syntheticCopyTrading.ts` (declared 7D override + `reportedRoi`), `src/services/copyTrading/kseniaReportedWeek.ts` (writes the three visible fields, anchored to the week), NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/copyMarketplaceSettles.test.ts`, `src/services/copyTrading/__tests__/kseniaReportedWeek.test.ts` (rewritten to the new contract), NEW `scripts/qa-copy-loading-and-ksenia.cjs`, evidence in `docs/qa/copy-loading-ksenia/`.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** Copy button, the eligibility rule, following/subscriptions, Copy Trading design, Futures, Wallet, engines #123/#149. No balance, AUM, follower PnL or fee changed.
- **TESTED.** Backend `tsc` PASS, frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS. Whole repo compared test by test against `main` (`03fc3e6`) in the same tree, both sides built: **126 failing before, 126 after — zero new, zero fixed**.
- **NOT VERIFIED.** No production check — I cannot hold an authenticated production session, so the reproduction is the local harness against the real compiled server code, not against the deployed instance. Two things follow from that and should be checked on the deployed site: whether the hang there is the same `settled` path this fixes (it reproduces exactly and is the only unsettled path left in the code), and that production's model clock is still inside 2026-09-13 → 2026-09-19, because the 61.9% is anchored to that week.
- **NOT MERGED**, as instructed.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — «Выйти» reached the sign-in page instead of the home page (same branch, PR #151)

- **CI caught a real defect, and it was not in Copy Trading.** The `copy-trading-card-regression` workflow went red on `9b5bef6` at `scripts/qa-copy-last-good-browser.cjs:434`, which asserts that signing out lands on `/`. Reproduced locally, then run unchanged against a **built `origin/main` (52dce28)** worktree, where it passes — so it was this branch's to answer.
- **WHAT IT ACTUALLY WAS.** `App` holds no state, so the JSX it returns is built once, at load. The root route decided its element inside that JSX: `element={getToken() ? <Navigate to={defaultTradingPath()} /> : <HomePage />}`. A visitor who arrived signed in froze `<Navigate to="/futures">` into the route table for the life of the tab. `handleLogout` cleared the token, navigated to `/`, met the frozen redirect, went to the terminal, and the terminal's own guard sent them to `/login?next=/futures`. Traced by instrumenting `history.pushState`/`replaceState`: `PUSH /` → `REPLACE /futures` → `REPLACE /login?next=%2Ffutures`.
- **WHY IT WAS GREEN ON `main`, said plainly rather than re-pinned.** The same three navigations happen there. The check passed because Playwright happened to observe the intermediate `/` for one frame. This branch settles the store update and the navigation together, that frame disappeared, and a long-standing defect stopped hiding. Nothing here made logout worse; it made it visible.
- **FIXED WHERE EVERY OTHER ROUTE ALREADY GETS IT RIGHT.** `RootEntry` reads the session inside a component, so it is read again on every render. `RequireAuth` and `RedirectIfAuthed` already worked this way; `/` was the only route reading `getToken()` while the table was built.
- **GUARDED FOR THE WHOLE TABLE, not just `/`.** NEW `frontend/src/lib/__tests__/rootRouteReadsTokenAtRender.test.ts` fails if any route element calls `getToken()`. Verified both ways: 3 passed here, and 2 of the 3 fail when run against `main`'s `App.tsx` — it would have caught this.
- **AND THE WORKFLOW THAT SHOULD HAVE BEEN WATCHING.** `frontend/src/App.tsx` and `frontend/src/components/Nav.tsx` were not in `copy-trading-card-regression.yml`'s trigger paths, although its browser QA signs in, routes away and back, and signs out. A change to either left the workflow unrun and the logout assertion unchecked. Both are now triggers, with the new guard, and the guard runs in the jest step.
- **RE-VERIFIED AFTER THE FIX.** `qa-copy-last-good-browser.cjs` PASS, `findings: []`. `qa-copy-cards-trades-avatar.cjs` `status: PASS`, `findings: []`. `qa-copy-loading-and-ksenia.cjs` PASS, `findings: []` — Ksenia still `+61.9%` under «ROI за отчётную неделю» at the later model date. The workflow's seven jest suites: 75 passed. Frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS.
- **NO NEW FAILURES FROM THE ROUTING CHANGE.** All nine suites that read `App.tsx` were run on this branch and on built `main`: the same five fail and the same four pass on both.
- **NOT TOUCHED.** No Copy Trading data, design, Copy button, eligibility, following, Futures, Wallet or engine change. The only behaviour that changed is where the sign-out link lands.
- **NOT MERGED**, as instructed.

### 2026-09-19 — Claude — Futures terminal: a calm book, the layout the owner asked for, and 7-day sorting (`claude/futures-terminal-calm-book-and-layout`)

- **CUT FROM FRESH `main` AT `78a5a04`.** Futures terminal UI only. No trading engine, execution, balances, PnL math, wallet ledger, margin logic, order matching, Copy Trading or admin change.
- **THE TARGET WAS MEASURED FROM THE OWNER'S OWN VIDEO, not guessed.** They supplied a screen recording of Binance Futures. Cropping its order book and diffing consecutive frames over 2.0s at 30fps: **5 repaints — 2.5 per second, mean gap 425 ms — and between them the panel is bit-identical** (max single-frame change 2.82%). That is the whole character of a calm book: it changes two or three times a second and is completely still in between.
- **NEW HARNESS, because the existing one could not see the fault.** `scripts/qa-orderbook-motion.cjs` counts DOM work, and a CSS transition makes no DOM writes at all — the ladder can be perfectly stable in the DOM and never hold still on screen. NEW `scripts/qa-orderbook-calm.cjs` captures every compositor paint over CDP, crops the panel, resamples to 30fps (the reference is a 30fps recording, so both are the same measurement) and diffs. It carries a **control region that must report zero**; an earlier version recorded with Playwright's `recordVideo` and the control reported 8 changed frames with a 99.8% peak — encoder keyframes, not the app — so the capture was replaced rather than the threshold tuned.
- **1. THE BOOK — ROOT CAUSE, AND IT WAS NOT THE DATA.** The store already coalesced to one publish per 300 ms. The noise was a **260 ms CSS ease on every depth bar**: twenty bars still travelling when the next update restarted them. **`TerminalAccountPanel.css` loads last on Futures and re-declared a 140 ms ease**, so removing it from `ReferenceFuturesTerminal.css` alone changed nothing — the measurement caught that: ladder CONTENT changed 2.33/s while its PIXELS changed 4.22/s, so nearly half the motion carried no new information.
- **MEASURED, before → after, same seeded replay, same instrument:** whole-page compositor paints in ~6 s **249 → 28**; ladder repaints **5.39/s → 2.17/s** (Binance 2.5); ladder gap **186 ms → 452 ms** (Binance 425 ms); pixels now equal content (2.17 vs 2.33), i.e. essentially every repaint carries new information. **Re-measured on the merged head** and it holds: 26 paints, **2.30/s**, 450 ms gap, control still 0. A six-second window means one repaint either way moves the last decimal, so the honest claim is *about 2.2–2.3 per second*, not a four-digit figure. `FLUSH_MS` 300 → **400** to match the measured cadence; it is display-only and cannot reach execution.
- **THE FAIL-BANNER THE OWNER PHOTOGRAPHED IS GONE, and nothing true was hidden.** A book that has not arrived is not a fault: waiting now draws a **quiet placeholder ladder** — real row geometry, em dashes, no colour — so the layout is already correct when levels land. A book that HAD data and stopped, and a book that is gone, are still named; they are drawn as a muted line with a small coloured dot instead of a tinted band. `trade.bookConnecting` no longer appears over an empty panel.
- **2. THE COLLAPSE CONTROL.** The collapsed drawing-rail tab was pinned to the chart's bottom-left corner (`bottom:10px`), hard against the time axis and the bottom tab row. It now sits at the chart's **vertical middle** (`top:50%` + half-height translate), as the owner marked on their screenshot and as Bybit does. Measured at **56.1 / 57.7 / 57.7 / 59.6 %** down the chart at 1920×1080, 1664×900, 1440×900, 1366×768. The phone layout keeps its horizontal strip.
- **3. THE BOTTOM PANEL OPENS OPEN.** `useCompactAccountPanel` folded itself whenever the account was verifiably empty — which is every new account — so the terminal greeted the owner with a full-height chart and the tab row collapsed to 44px. Folding is now a user action and only a user action. Verified open on cold load, hard reload and route leave/return at all four widths; the control still folds on demand (chart 553 → 734px at 1440) and is still offered only while both reads are verifiably empty, so a panel holding live positions cannot be folded away. Deliberately **not persisted**: a remembered fold is how the old default would come back.
- **4. THE CHOOSER LAYOUT.** The filter row was clipped through its own text, there was a dead band under it, and the two sort headings sat 4–10px off the columns they name. Fixed with rules scoped to `.futures-market-chooser` **carrying the terminal sheets' own root classes** — plain descendant selectors lost to the studio sheet's five-class rule and the chooser kept the 212px rail's track map inside a 330px panel. The heading row now shares the rows' five-track grid, and both reserve the same scrollbar gutter via the same two properties rather than a magic padding (`index.css` sets `scrollbar-width: thin` globally, which makes Chromium ignore any nearby `::-webkit-scrollbar` width).
- **5. SEVEN-DAY SORTING, ON REAL DATA.** Two chips — gainers and losers — in the chooser's filter row. The figure is `market.changePercent7d` off the **shared asset catalogue the Markets page already sorts by**; the venue's ticker publishes no 7d field and none was invented. A contract the catalogue does not cover keeps `null` and sorts last in both directions. The catalogue is subscribed **only while a 7-day sort is active**. While it is on, the change column shows the 7-day figure and its heading reads `7д %`, so the order on screen is the order the control claims. Verified at all four widths: gainers `AVAX, SOL, ATOM`, losers `NEAR, DOGE, XRP`, both exactly the expected ranking. **Not in the left rail** — at 212px the second chip clipped to «▼ 7».
- **TESTS I BROKE AND WHAT I DID ABOUT THEM.** Five suites were green and went red, and none was made green by weakening it. `futuresDepth` hard-coded `300`; `FLUSH_MS` is now exported and the tests advance by it, so they assert coalescing rather than a literal. `orderBookCalmMotion` asserted a *short* ease — the premise the owner rejected — and is now a sweep over **every** terminal sheet for any depth-bar transition, which is exactly the check that would have caught the 140 ms rule surviving in a later sheet; its connecting-chip assertion became a placeholder-ladder assertion. `terminalAccountPanel` encoded the old fold-by-default and now pins the new contract plus a new assertion that the fold is only OFFERED once both reads are verifiably empty. `futuresPairSort` needed the catalogue module supplied by name. `cryptoCatalogue`'s SHA pin on `FuturesPairList.tsx` was re-pinned `00f2140b…` → `4be3a5a6…`, **with a new test beside it** asserting the catalogue is read for one field, through one call, and can never add, remove or rename a market — because this is the first edit that makes that module import the catalogue at all.
- **Material files.** `frontend/src/lib/futuresDepth.ts`, `frontend/src/components/FuturesReferenceBook.tsx`, `frontend/src/components/FuturesPairList.tsx` + `.css`, `frontend/src/components/TerminalChart.css`, `frontend/src/lib/useCompactAccountPanel.ts`, `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/ReferenceFuturesTerminal.css`, `frontend/src/pages/trade-terminal/TerminalAccountPanel.css`, `frontend/src/pages/FuturesPage.tsx`, `frontend/src/pages/TradePage.tsx`, seven locale files, five test files, NEW `scripts/qa-orderbook-calm.cjs` and `scripts/qa-futures-layout.cjs`, evidence in `docs/qa/orderbook-calm/` and `docs/qa/futures-layout/`.
- **PRESERVED FROM CODEX.** Everything in `main`, including the #146/#149 native engine and the #148 Futures geometry work, is untouched; no Codex file was reverted or rewritten.
- **REBASED FORWARD DURING THE TASK.** Cut from `main` at `78a5a04`; the owner merged PR #151 while this was in flight, so `main` moved to `faf58f8` and it was merged in rather than the branch being recut. The only conflict was this file — two entries appended at the same place — and both are kept, #151's first. Nothing in #151's Copy Trading work was altered.

The same **29 suites fail identically on both sides: zero new, zero fixed.** The four extra passing tests are the ones added here. Backend `tsc` PASS, frontend `tsc` PASS, `npm run build` PASS.
